- **p-code-review-correctness**: Invoked by p-code-review to run the correctness / code-quality channel across the entire working tree (no diff). Reads representative source files, greps for likely bug patterns, and returns findings as strict JSON. Not intended for standalone use — invoke the p-code-review skill instead, which orchestrates this agent alongside the security and reuse channels. ([Jayant2907/p-code-review](https://github.com/Jayant2907/p-code-review/blob/HEAD/agents/p-code-review-correctness.md))
- **p-code-review-reuse**: Invoked by p-code-review to run the reuse / duplication channel across the entire working tree (no diff). Finds copy-pasted logic, dead code, over-abstraction, and modules that would benefit from consolidation. Returns strict JSON. Not intended for standalone use — invoke the p-code-review skill instead, which orchestrates this agent alongside the correctness and security channels. ([Jayant2907/p-code-review](https://github.com/Jayant2907/p-code-review/blob/HEAD/agents/p-code-review-reuse.md))
- **p-code-review-security**: Invoked by p-code-review to run the security channel across the entire working tree (no diff). Adversarial read of the codebase — untrusted-input flows, auth gaps, secret exposure, unsafe deps. Returns strict JSON. Inherits the model selected in the invoking Claude session. Not intended for standalone use — invoke the p-code-review skill instead. ([Jayant2907/p-code-review](https://github.com/Jayant2907/p-code-review/blob/HEAD/agents/p-code-review-security.md))

([Druthulu/BFM-decomp](https://github.com/Druthulu/BFM-decomp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pa-claude.md) · ★99)

- **pa-expert**: Executes one PHASE_PLAN task in a fresh context. Reads the plan's context, decides, briefs coders and retrievers, writes the task log and summary, returns a plain report. - **pa-planner**: Drafts one generation or phase plan for Claude (the partner) in a PA 3.2 project, outside plan mode, following the pa-planning skill. Never writes the plan files, never commits a plan, never talks to the developer. ([Druthulu/BFM-decomp](https://github.com/Druthulu/BFM-decomp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pa-planner.md) · ★99)
- **pa-thinker**: One-shot deep answer to one hard question for Claude (the partner), on Opus 5.5 at max effort (PA 3.2, mod on). - **pair-implementer**: Implements a single Pair user story within the authoring chain — builds a fresh story test-first above its base (tests and code together, an informal unrecorded self-review before publishing) or, on a run already carrying a sealed `a0`, above its RED snapshot, and publishes its one PR (implement-phase), or makes a sealed remediation contract pass inside fixScope and updates the PR (green-fix). Spawned per stage by the batch engine; each stage is a versioned phase skill. Never a verifier, never merges. ([foomakers/pair](https://github.com/foomakers/pair/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pair-implementer.md) · ★9 · 7 copies)
- **pair-programmer**: Top-level agent launched using the claude-agent pair-programmer CLI command. General-purpose engineer for direct user collaboration. Never spawn as a sub-agent. ([platformplatform/PlatformPlatform](https://github.com/platformplatform/PlatformPlatform/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pair-programmer.md) · ★447 · 2 copies)
- **pair-programmer**: Use this agent when you need collaborative problem-solving for programming challenges, want to explore multiple solution approaches before coding, or need guidance on choosing the best implementation strategy. This agent excels at breaking down complex problems and providing strategic technical advice. ([NikiforovAll/claude-code-rules](https://github.com/NikiforovAll/claude-code-rules/blob/HEAD/plugins/handbook-extras/agents/pair-programmer.md) · ★143 · 2 copies)
- **pair-programmer**: Live thinking partner — Kodelyth. A decade-seasoned senior engineer who thinks out loud with you before you write a single line. Challenges assumptions, spots wrong approaches early, asks the questions you haven't thought of yet, and makes sure the implementation plan is sound before any code is committed. The cheapest bug is the one caught before coding starts. Use before implementing anything non-trivial. ([sifxprime/kodelyth-ecc](https://github.com/sifxprime/kodelyth-ecc/blob/HEAD/agents/pair-programmer.md) · ★11)
- **pair-programmer**: Contrarian sidecar that challenges assumptions and surfaces alternatives during implementation. Does not write code. ([firefly-events/plugin-hive](https://github.com/firefly-events/plugin-hive/blob/HEAD/hive/agents/pair-programmer.md) · ★2)
- **pair-programmer**: A collaborative, pragmatic software engineering partner who values clean code, TDD, and thorough research. ([jakebrinkmann/.dotfiles](https://github.com/jakebrinkmann/.dotfiles/blob/HEAD/dots/copilot/.copilot/agents/pair-programmer.agent.md) · ★2)
- **pair-programmer**: 페어 프로그래밍 네비게이터. 사용자(드라이버)가 TDD 기반 프로덕션 코드를 직접 작성하도록 방향 제시·패턴 안내·코드 리뷰 담당. BE(Kotlin/Spring Boot 헥사고날)·FE(React 19/TypeScript) 모두 커버. 코드를 직접 작성하지 않는다. ([bangddong/switch-job-quest](https://github.com/bangddong/switch-job-quest/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pair-programmer.md))
- **pair-programmer**: Collaborative coding partner — works through implementation with the user, explaining decisions and exploring alternatives. Use when pair programming, learning a codebase, or working through complex logic. ([kennedym-ds/claudecode-orchestrator](https://github.com/kennedym-ds/claudecode-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/plugins/cc-sdlc-core/.claude/agents/pair-programmer.md))
- **pair-programmer**: Pair programming assistant for the Python service (services/processing/) of the Institutional Knowledge project. Invoke during active implementation of Python tasks. The developer leads; this agent assists with the current task in scope only. ([matthew-was/institutional-knowledge](https://github.com/matthew-was/institutional-knowledge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pair-programmer.md))
- **pair-programmer**: Collaborative implementation partner that develops features incrementally while making non-obvious code-shape decisions understandable. ([Stringy/configs](https://github.com/Stringy/configs/blob/HEAD/.config/opencode/agents/pair-programmer.md))
- **pair-programmer-reviewer**: Collaborative code review partner specializing in implementation analysis and solution architecture. Reviews completed code for quality, security, and performance. Evaluates multiple approaches with trade-offs. Provides expert recommendations ensuring solutions fully address requirements. Ideal for post-implementation reviews, refactoring planning, and architecture decisions. Acts as thoughtful pair programmer offering alternatives and improvements. ([SacredTexts/goldy](https://github.com/SacredTexts/goldy/blob/HEAD/agents/pair-programmer-reviewer.md))
- **pair-programming**: You guide interactive pair programming sessions. AI is Navigator, user is Driver. You operate autonomously. ([DGouron/review-flow](https://github.com/DGouron/review-flow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pair-programming.md) · ★44)
- **pair-proposer**: Reads the generated option pools and proposes pairs of option ids that might be related, writing one candidates file for shard_candidates.py to split. High recall, no decisions, deletes nothing. Used by the creative-problem-solving pipeline. ([yaniv-golan/creative-problem-solving](https://github.com/yaniv-golan/creative-problem-solving/blob/HEAD/creative-problem-solving/agents/pair-proposer.md) · ★1)
- **pair-red-contract-verifier**: Independent validator of a Pair acceptance contract, and its sealer. Reproduces every witness and control against the unfixed base, re-derives the inventory from the authoritative producer, emits every gap it finds in one typed rejection — and, when the contract holds, runs the deterministic sealer script in the same execution and returns the snapshot. A separate role from the author and the fixer; never repairs, never edits production. ([foomakers/pair](https://github.com/foomakers/pair/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pair-red-contract-verifier.md) · ★9 · 7 copies)
- **pair-reviewer**: Independent final verifier of a single Pair PR head — custody by script (red-snapshot.mjs verify-chain), the fixers' evidence re-run, the sealed witnesses and controls, every prior blocking finding with an explicit transition, the delta and its consumers, cross-group interactions, the adopted risk-tier review passes — into one finding set with stable ids, one verdict, the head reviewed and a readiness bound to the remote head. Publishes the one first review or the one synthesis idempotently. Reviews only from the story, the PR, the code and the cycle's evidence — never the author's checkpoint… ([foomakers/pair](https://github.com/foomakers/pair/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pair-reviewer.md) · ★9 · 7 copies)
- **pair-reviewer**: Independent code review with a second engine family; falls back to reviewing it yourself if that engine does not respond. Use after implementing changes for an adversarial second opinion. ([T50-Systems/lider](https://github.com/T50-Systems/lider/blob/HEAD/plugins/lider/agents/pair-reviewer.md))
- **pair-verifier**: Pair programming verifier agent for code review and test validation within a Claude Teams session. Works with a pair-coder teammate: waits for implementation completion signal, then verifies code quality, test coverage, and correctness. ([jleechanorg/jleechan-skills](https://github.com/jleechanorg/jleechan-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pair-verifier.md) · ★6)
- **pair-worker-subagents-with-observer-watchdog-agents**: Enable observer agents and add an `observer:` field to a worker subagent's frontmatter so a read-only watchdog sees every tool call and result and sends course-correcting reports the moment the worker games tests, deletes/weakens them, or drifts from its constraints. - **palantir**: Researches a question on the open web (an advisory, a claim to fact-check, practice beyond the training cutoff). Not for auditing this repo's code, not for a library's own docs (context7). ([techmefr/mentis](https://github.com/techmefr/mentis/blob/HEAD/agents/palantir.md))
- **Palantir-Agent-Stack-Sessions**: Agent Stack은 한 번의 chat completion보다 오래 지속되는 업무를 session, typed context items와 event/state machine으로 연결하는 기반이다. 여러 참여자가 다른 앱에서 같은 task에 기여하고 agent가 검토를 기다렸다가 입력이 오면 이어갈 수 있다. ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Agent-Stack-Sessions.md))
- **Palantir-AI-FDE-and-Skills**: AI FDE는 Foundry를 대화형으로 조작하는 개발 agent다. 별도 service account가 아니라 현재 사용자의 session과 권한으로 실행하며 AIP enablement가 필요하다. Ontology 변경에는 Global Branching enablement가 권장된다. 코드를 async function으로 배포하는 [[Palantir-Pro-Code-Agents]]와 실행 모델이 다르다. ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-AI-FDE-and-Skills.md))
- **Palantir-Assist-Content-and-Context**: AIP Assist는 플랫폼 사용을 지원하는 대화형 도구다. Default는 platform/developer/custom source를 고르고, 전용 documentation mode와 custom chatbot을 선택할 수 있다. AIP enablement와 해당 기능의 enrollment availability를 확인한다. ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Assist-Content-and-Context.md))
- **Palantir-Chatbot-Application-State**: Model context, tool 입력/출력과 UI state를 각각 정한다. LLM이 대답한 사실과 필요한 variable이 갱신된 사실은 다르다. 2025년 4월 개편 이력과 2026년 10월 전용 문서의 계약을 함께 구분한다. ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Chatbot-Application-State.md))
- **Palantir-Chatbot-Retrieval-and-Citations**: Chatbot Studio는 이전 Agent Studio 명칭의 interactive assistant 구성 도구다. Instructions, tool/variable description은 compiled system prompt를 만든다. LLM에 주는 context와 실행 가능한 tool을 별도로 설계한다. Model은 enrollment에서 활성화한 일부만 선택할 수 있다. ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Chatbot-Retrieval-and-Citations.md))
- **Palantir-Chatbot-Sessions-and-Distribution**: Interactive chatbot을 Function, API, Workshop 또는 Marketplace로 배포할 때 같은 prompt가 같은 실행 환경을 보장하지 않는다. Saved/published version, resource dependency, session input과 client tool 지원을 함께 고정한다. ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Chatbot-Sessions-and-Distribution.md))
- **Palantir-Chatbot-Tools-and-Commands**: Chatbot tool은 LLM이 control flow를 정하고 입력값을 구성해 호출하는 경로다. 다만 Action과 Function은 application variable의 deterministic input(string과 object set 입력만)을, Command는 beta configuration에서 preset 값이나 string/object set application variable을 쓰도록 입력을 고정할 수 있다. Action은 Ontology edit, Object query는 허용한 type/property의 filter/aggregation/inspection/link traversal, Function은 published Logic을 포함한 함수 호출을 담당한다. Update app ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Chatbot-Tools-and-Commands.md))
- **Palantir-Foundry-Agents**: - [[Palantir-Pro-Code-Agents|함수로 배포하는 Pro-code agent]] - [[Palantir-AI-FDE-and-Skills|대화형 AI FDE와 재사용 지침]] - [[Palantir-Agent-Stack-Sessions|Agent Stack의 장기 session과 event]] - [[Palantir-Chatbot-Application-State|Chatbot의 context, 변수와 UI trigger]] - [[Palantir-Chatbot-Retrieval-and-Citations|Retrieval과 citation 계약]] - [[Palantir-Chatbot-Tools-and-Commands|Tool mode와 client command]] - [[Palantir-C ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Foundry-Agents.md))
- **Palantir-Generated-Applications-and-Seed-Data**: Pilot은 요구에서 Ontology, design specification과 React OSDK 앱을 만드는 개발 workflow다. 2026년 3월 beta 도입 계약이며 생성된 앱의 업무 정확성, 인가와 배포 성공을 직접 시험한 기록은 아니다. Pilot은 2026-10-29 GA로 발표됐으며 현재 문서 기준의 발행 절차는 [[Palantir-Pilot-Application-and-Widget-Release|Pilot 앱과 위젯의 발행 계약]]에서 다룬다. ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Generated-Applications-and-Seed-Data.md))
- **Palantir-Pro-Code-Agents**: Pro-code agent는 Code Repository의 custom agent logic을 asynchronous Foundry function으로 배포하는 경로다. Claude Agent SDK, OpenAI Agents SDK와 Google ADK template을 제공하며 OSDK, Ontology MCP와 Palantir MCP를 사용한다. 확인일 기준 beta이며 실제 agent 생성, 권한과 장애를 시험한 기록은 아니다. ([dc-choi/interview](https://github.com/dc-choi/interview/blob/HEAD/tech/ai-engineering/palantir/foundry/agents/Palantir-Pro-Code-Agents.md))

Verifies every numeric/empirical claim traces to docs/research/paper-full-2.0.md and the evaluation DB, and that spin-offs introduce no original claims. Read-only plus the project's existing validators. ([liammagee/machinespirits-eval](https://github.com/liammagee/machinespirits-eval/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/paper-claim-auditor.md))
- **paper-clarity**: Review academic papers for clarity. Checks that a reader unfamiliar with the specific subfield can follow the paper. - **paper-code-auditor**: Code audit agent. Hunts for gaps between what the paper CLAIMS the code does and what the code ACTUALLY does — specification errors, standard-error mistakes, absence errors, variable construction mismatches, undisclosed manipulations, and identification-assumption gaps. Use only when a replication-code directory is provided. Report every paper-code gap you can demonstrate; do not pad the list to hit a count. ([Felpix-Studios/peer-review](https://github.com/Felpix-Studios/peer-review/blob/HEAD/agents/paper-code-auditor.md) · ★3)
- **paper-code-checker**: Paper-versus-code checker of an agentic-review literature review. Checks the implementation claims the analysis makes about each core paper against the paper's own code, with commit-pinned line permalinks (conforms, differs, not found). Started by the agentic-review orchestrator during the code audit. ([titouanlegourrierec/agentic-review](https://github.com/titouanlegourrierec/agentic-review/blob/HEAD/plugins/agentic-review/agents/paper-code-checker.md) · ★1)

- **paper-copilot**: 论文写作 Agent。逐章撰写高质量学术文本，专注纯学术质量，不背负 AIGC 表达优化任务。使用骨架常驻 + search_blocks + query_data_facts 注入上下文，每章经 Checker 体系审查后交付。 ([BH2-4/paper-craft-pipeline](https://github.com/BH2-4/paper-craft-pipeline/blob/HEAD/agents/paper-copilot.md) · ★1)
- **paper-correctness**: Review academic papers for technical correctness. Checks that definitions, theorems, algorithms, and technical claims are mathematically and logically sound. - **paper-crawler**: Collects and classifies research papers from DBLP and OpenAlex APIs for literature surveys ([andrehuang/academic-writing-agents](https://github.com/andrehuang/academic-writing-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/paper-crawler.md) · ★239 · 5 copies)
- **paper-critic**: Adversarial auditor for LaTeX papers. - **paper-critic**: Use this agent to read one paper adversarially: it separates what the paper claims from what its evidence supports, finds methodological holes (weak baselines, unfair comparisons, leakage, missing variance, cherry-picked settings), checks whether the conclusions follow, and returns a critique the student can use to position their own work or to decide how much to trust the paper. - **paper-critic**: Reviews a generated item set for groundedness, ambiguity, duplication, difficulty mislabeling, distractor quality, and time budget. Returns a per-item verdict. - **paper-data-auditor**: 论文数据审查 Agent。审查数据一致性（语义等价）、数据完备性、捏造检测。支持完备性审查（Gate 1）和准确性审查（Gate 2）两种模式，由各主 Agent 的门控流程触发调用。 ([BH2-4/paper-craft-pipeline](https://github.com/BH2-4/paper-craft-pipeline/blob/HEAD/agents/paper-data-auditor.md) · ★1)

- **paper-drafter**: Use this agent to draft ONE assigned section of a publication bundle manuscript at Stage 10, against an explicit brief from the lead. - **paper-formatter**: 论文排版与终审交付 Agent。负责 Markdown 就绪性检查、单主链排版（minimax-docx 为主、pandoc fallback）、docx 成品校验、终审报告。 ([BH2-4/paper-craft-pipeline](https://github.com/BH2-4/paper-craft-pipeline/blob/HEAD/agents/paper-formatter.md) · ★1)
- **paper-hunter**: 논문 수집 전문가. 기본은 주요 학회 6개(NeurIPS·AAAI·ICLR·ICML·ACL·EMNLP)의 accepted 논문만 수집하고 `papers/metadata/&lt;Venue&gt;/&lt;Year&gt;/`로 저장한다. workshop·ACL Findings·arXiv preprint 등 non-whitelist 논문과 arXiv 키워드 수집은 `hunt.py --include-arxiv` 플래그(PLAN.md에 include_arxiv=true 로 기록)를 받아야만 `papers/metadata/etc/&lt;Year&gt;/`에 opt-in으로 수집한다. OpenReview·ACL Anthology·arXiv를 venue별 소스 전략으로 스캔하고, 중복 제거(정규화 제목 + arXiv ID + anthology ID + openreview ID), 증분 cursor 관리, 메타데이터를 `papers/metadata/&lt;Venue|etc&gt;/&lt;Year&gt;/&lt;slug&gt;.raw.md`에 저장한다. "논문 수집", "arxiv 검색", "OpenReview 논문", "venue 스캔", "새 논문 찾기" 관련 요청 시 호출된다. 본격적 요약은 paper-summarizer가 담당한다. ([youai058/research_hub](https://github.com/youai058/research_hub/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/paper-hunter.md))

([hasantahabagci/agentic-paper2code](https://github.com/hasantahabagci/agentic-paper2code/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/paper-implementer.md))
